- **method-selector**: Choose the right research method or methods for a study. - **method-self-distill**: 在一个 Agent/工具任务已经完成或明确失败后，如果出现长路径、反复试错、多个失败工具、明显绕路、用户纠正后才成功或希望把本次教训转成下次更短行动路径时使用。基于可观察的任务、工具动作、Evidence/结果和用户反馈进行独立 Method 蒸馏；重点学习“如何从轨迹抽出判别方法”，不保存或复述原始思维链，不把候选自动升级成规则或强制工作流。 ([deyi2026/llm-first-loop](https://github.com/deyi2026/llm-first-loop/tree/HEAD/skills/method-self-distill) · ★1)

- **method-self-distill-repo-api**: Repo/API Method teacher exemplar。用于教模型从真实并行代码改动事故中提炼“当前定义 + 当前 callsite 才是真值、旧 grep 行号只是线索”的方法。样板来自 2026-09-03 必填参数变更后基于旧 callsite 清单编辑，最终 full gate 暴露 missing keyword-only argument 的真实 LFL Experience。 ([deyi2026/llm-first-loop](https://github.com/deyi2026/llm-first-loop/tree/HEAD/skills/method-self-distill-repo-api) · ★1)
- **method-self-distill-root-cause**: Root Cause Method teacher exemplar。用于任务完成后，教模型怎样从真实根因事故中找“最早可区分事实”并压成可迁移诊断方法；样板来自 fresh CI 首次 wake 被 relative monotonic clock + 0.0 sentinel 错误限流的真实 LFL 事故。仅用于 Method 蒸馏实验，不是生产规则。 ([deyi2026/llm-first-loop](https://github.com/deyi2026/llm-first-loop/tree/HEAD/skills/method-self-distill-root-cause) · ★1)

- **method-tab**: The unit economics of a feature — cost per use, who pays, what the cap is, and at what volume the design stops working. Refuses "it's cheap" without the arithmetic, and refuses a forecast where a forecast would be a lie. Utiliser pour une séance de travail, une roadmap, un avis sur une décision, une livraison de code, une recette, un dry run, un « go solo » ou une reprise sur Ultima, Rénoboost, RenoDevis, Artidispo ou Artisan Builder. ([canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang](https://github.com/canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang/tree/HEAD/plugins/market/Knowledge/bio-research-guided/skills/methylation))
- **methylation-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating a methylation caller on PacBio HiFi reads with known ground-truth methylation states (PCR-treated unmethylated vs. M.SssI-methylated controls), e.g., to reproduce ccsmeth vs. HK model read-level accuracies or tune an ambiguous-call filter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **methylation-accuracy-correlation-validation**: Use when a new methylation-calling or methylation-phasing output (bedmethyl, per-haplotype frequencies, DMR lists) must be benchmarked against BS-seq or nanopore ground truth to confirm accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-accuracy-correlation-validation))
- **methylation-aggregation**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/methylation-aggregation) · ★745)
- **methylation-aggregation**: Build comprehensive DNA methylation maps by aggregating WGBS (Whole Genome Bisulfite Sequencing) data across multiple ENCODE experiments, donors, and labs. Use when the user wants to answer "where is DNA methylated/unmethylated in my tissue?" by combining per-CpG methylation data into tissue-level methylation profiles. Handles coverage filtering, identifies hypomethylated regions (HMRs) and partially methylated domains (PMDs), and manages cross-lab variation. ([ammawla/encode-toolkit](https://github.com/ammawla/encode-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/methylation-aggregation) · ★20 · 4 copies)
- **methylation-aggregation-ammawla-encode-toolkit**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/methylation-aggregation-ammawla-encode-toolkit) · ★745)
- **methylation-alignment-mode-comparison**: Use when comparing DNA methylation read aligners (4-letter vs 3-letter) on TAPS/bisulfite-free reads, especially for CT/GA-rich regions where 3-letter C-&gt;T conversion collapses mapping power. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-alignment-mode-comparison))
- **methylation-analysis**: Workflow for methylation alignment or calling, DMR analysis, methylation QC, and locus-level interpretation. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioclaw_hub/methylation-analysis) · ★224 · 3 copies)
- **methylation-analysis**: Analyze DNA methylation data from Illumina 450K/EPIC arrays or bisulfite sequencing. Computes differential methylation between groups, identifies differentially methylated regions (DMRs), annotates CpGs, and visualizes results. ([MDhewei/bioinfor-claw](https://github.com/MDhewei/bioinfor-claw/tree/HEAD/multiomics-data-analysis/methylation-analysis) · ★65)
- **methylation-analysis-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `Methylation analysis` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/epigenomics-and-chromatin/methylation-analysis-starter) · ★46)
- **methylation-and-epigenomics**: Use when analyzing methylation (human arrays, bisulfite, EM-seq, ONT), ChIP-seq or ATAC-seq peaks, human episignatures, or cell composition, in any organism. Not for expression analysis (rna-seq-and-splicing). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/methylation-and-epigenomics))
- **methylation-array-probe-preprocessing**: Use when the input is a raw Illumina 450K methylation beta-value matrix (probes x samples, e.g. from GEO GSE36278) containing sex-chromosome probes, SNP-overlapping probes, missing values, outliers, and low-variance rows prior to cola consensus partitioning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-array-probe-preprocessing))
- **methylation-based-detection**: Analyzes cfDNA methylation patterns for cancer detection using cfMeDIP-seq or bisulfite sequencing with MethylDackel. Identifies cancer-specific methylation signatures and performs tissue-of-origin deconvolution. Use when using methylation biomarkers for early cancer detection or minimal residual disease. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/liquid-biopsy-methylation-based-detection) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **methylation-based-detection**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Oncology/Liquid_Biopsy/methylation-based-detection) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **methylation-based-read-haplotagging**: Use when ONT reads with MM/ML methylation tags have been SNV-phased (HapCUT2) and WhatsHap-haplotagged, but many reads remain untagged and SNV phase blocks need to be chained across homozygous gaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-based-read-haplotagging))
- **methylation-batch-effect-detection**: Use when you have loaded a normalized beta-valued methylation matrix (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/methylation-batch-effect-detection))
- **methylation-benchmark-metric-computation**: Use when nanopore methylation tool outputs (per-site methylation frequencies, e.g. freq-perCG augmented BED files) must be quantitatively benchmarked against known expected methylation frequencies from a control mixture dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-benchmark-metric-computation))
- **methylation-bismark-alignment**: Bisulfite sequencing read alignment using Bismark with bowtie2/hisat2. Handles genome preparation and produces BAM files with methylation information. Use when aligning WGBS, RRBS, or other bisulfite-converted sequencing reads to a reference genome. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/methylation-analysis-bismark-alignment) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **methylation-calibration-curve-fitting**: Use when bisulfite amplicon/pyrosequencing data include standard controls with known methylation percentages (e.g. AMP = 0/25/50/75/100%) and apparent post-PCR methylation levels per CpG need bias-corrected calibration curves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-calibration-curve-fitting))
- **methylation-call-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when evaluating m6A per-read predictions from ONT nanopore eventalign data and you need to restrict classification to reads above an average base-quality cutoff before scoring per-position accuracy against reference labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-call-accuracy-evaluation))

- **methylation-calling**: Extract methylation calls from Bismark BAM files using bismark_methylation_extractor. Generates per-cytosine reports for CpG, CHG, and CHH contexts. Use when extracting methylation levels from aligned bisulfite sequencing data for downstream analysis. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/methylation-analysis-methylation-calling) · ★104 · 2 copies)

- **methylation-calling-roc-auc-benchmarking**: Use when comparing nanopore methylation-detection tools on 6mA-containing site detection, especially when performance may depend on site length and the presence of co-occurring methylated CpGs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-calling-roc-auc-benchmarking))
- **methylation-clock**: Compute epigenetic age from DNA methylation arrays using PyAging clocks from GEO accessions or local files. ([ClawBio/ClawBio](https://github.com/ClawBio/ClawBio/tree/HEAD/skills/methylation-clock) · ★1.2k · 11 copies)
- **methylation-core-cross-tool-comparison**: Use when bisulfite sequencing reads (e.g. FASTA epiallele tables from DDO1/DAO amplicons) have been processed with both single-epiallele tools and MethCoresProfiler, and the goal is to demonstrate that epiallele frequency distributions alone miss stable methylation cores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-core-cross-tool-comparison))
- **methylation-core-significance-testing-chi-square**: Use when bisulfite amplicon data (paired-end FASTQ, e.g. ENA PRJEB28662) has been reduced to binary epihaplotype tables of mCpG configurations and the goal is to detect stable phased mCpG combinations and trace epiallele families in complex DNA populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-core-significance-testing-chi-square))
- **methylation-core-statistical-annotation**: Use when amplicon bisulfite reads have been converted to binary epihaplotype matrices of phased CpG methylation states and the goal is to extract significant mCpG combinations, cores, clonality/entanglement indices, or track epiallele families across cell types or time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-core-statistical-annotation))
- **methylation-count-table-validation**: Use when reconstructing or testing the methylation-count extraction step of a MethylC-seq pipeline and confirming that output tables match a reference implementation, including CpG sites absent from the reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-count-table-validation))
- **methylation-coverage-correlation-analysis**: Use when you need to determine the minimum sequencing depth (e.g. 5x-50x) at which nanopore-based CpG methylation calls (nanoHiMe/nanopolish) still correlate with a bisulfite-based gold standard such as WGBS, or when planning MinION flowcell requirements. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-coverage-correlation-analysis))
- **methylation-cycle**: Methylation cycle analysis skill for ClawBio. Produces enzymatic activity profiles, Net Methylation Capacity (NMC), BH4 axis estimates, compound heterozygosity detection, and clinician-review genotype findings from raw SNP genotype data. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/clawbio/claw-methylation-cycle) · ★224 · 2 copies)
- **methylation-data-clustering**: Use when after merging methylation call files across all samples into a unified methylBase object (via unite()), when you need to assess whether biological replicates cluster together, identify unexpected sample groupings, or visualize global methylation similarity relationships before proceeding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/methylation-data-clustering))
- **methylation-density-comparison-analysis**: Use when you have Msuite methylation-call outputs (CpG/CpH meth.call files) from simulated benchmark reads with known preset methylation densities and need to quantify call accuracy per cytosine context. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-density-comparison-analysis))
- **methylation-differential-cpg**: Per-CpG differential methylation testing from bisulfite sequencing count data or beta-value matrices. Covers beta and M-value computation, coverage filtering, statistical tests (Welch t-test, Mann-Whitney, limma, DSS beta-binomial), multiple testing correction, and effect size calculation. Use when comparing methylation at individual CpG sites between experimental groups from WGBS, RRBS, or targeted bisulfite sequencing. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/methylation-analysis-differential-cpg-testing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **methylation-dmr-detection**: Differentially methylated region (DMR) detection using methylKit tiles, bsseq BSmooth, and DMRcate. Use when identifying contiguous genomic regions with methylation differences between experimental conditions or cell types. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/methylation-analysis-dmr-detection) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **methylation-effect-size-filtering**: Use when a MultiPower run on a multi-omic design with Methyl-seq (or another high-feature-count omic) yields an inflated optimal sample size driven by low-effect methylation features, and you want to test whether filtering boosts power. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/methylation-effect-size-filtering))
- **methylation-frequency-aggregation-from-per-site-calls**: Use when you have per-site methylation_rate TSVs from DeepPlant or Dorado calls on ONT R10.4 plant data and need genome-wide CHH methylation frequencies and Pearson correlation (r) vs BS-seq. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-frequency-aggregation-from-per-site-calls))
- **methylation-frequency-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have Dorado call_mods-style per-site methylation_rate TSV output plus BS-seq methylation frequencies and need to tune a score threshold or benchmark site-level methylation quantification accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-frequency-correlation-analysis))
- **methylation-frequency-regression-modeling**: Use when you have per-read methylation score files (ID/Chr/Pos/Strand/Score) from two or more nanopore 5mC tools, such as Megalodon and DeepSignal, and want a consensus per-site CpG methylation frequency that outperforms the individual tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-frequency-regression-modeling))
- **methylation-group-comparison-statistics**: Use when ab1 bisulfite sequencing PCR reads have already been individually analyzed for CpG methylation and you need to compare methylation percentages between sample groups (e.g. high- vs low-methylated DNA controls) with plots and significance tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-group-comparison-statistics))
- **methylation-hidden-markov-state-modeling**: Use when bisulfite sequencing data from a heterogeneous sample must be deconvolved into major allelic subpopulation methylation profiles over user-defined regions, without prior knowledge of subpopulation count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-hidden-markov-state-modeling))
- **methylation-hmm-score-computation**: Use when implementing or extending the GPU methylation score calculation step of Galaxy-methyl/f5c call-methylation, e.g. writing or tuning the CUDA HMM Fill_kernel over FAST5 signal-level data from basecalled Nanopore reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-hmm-score-computation))
- **methylation-hyper-hypo-classification**: Use when after calculating differential methylation across samples using calculateDiffMeth(), when you need to separately enumerate and extract hyper-methylated (increased methylation) versus hypo-methylated (decreased methylation) bases that meet both statistical significance (q-value &lt; 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/methylation-hyper-hypo-classification))
- **methylation-level-correction-evaluation**: Use when observed bisulfite amplicon/pyrosequencing methylation percentages in case and control samples need PCR-bias correction, and the stability or concordance of the resulting hyper/hypomethylation classifications must be assessed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-level-correction-evaluation))
- **methylation-level-numerical-inversion**: Use when fitted MethylCal INLA calibration models and formatted BWS-style methylation data are available and the apparent methylation level of case/control samples must be converted back to a corrected methylation degree per CpG-AMP combination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-level-numerical-inversion))
- **methylation-matrix-aggregation-across-samples**: Use when a completed per-sample (cf)MeDIP-seq methylation quantification run must be combined across many samples, groups, or batches into genome-bin x sample matrices with standardized region filtering and region-queryable output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-matrix-aggregation-across-samples))
- **methylation-methylkit**: DNA methylation analysis with methylKit in R. Import Bismark coverage files, filter by coverage, normalize samples, and perform statistical comparisons. Use when analyzing single-base methylation patterns, comparing samples, or preparing data for DMR detection. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/methylation-analysis-methylkit-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **methylation-model-input-construction**: Use when Nanopolish eventalign output must be converted into CHEUI Model 1 input files — per-read 9-mer signal vectors (100 values) plus distance vectors — for m6A (centered on A) or m5C (centered on C) read-level prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-model-input-construction))
- **methylation-modification-calling**: Use when you have plant ONT R10.4 POD5 reads with a sorted BAM and reference genome and need per-read or genome-wide 5mC calls in CHH (or CpG/CHG) contexts, particularly where Dorado's CHH accuracy is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-modification-calling))
- **methylation-motif-benchmarking-against-pacbio-reference**: Use when evaluating how many methylation motifs an ONT-native pipeline recovers de novo relative to a PacBio-characterized methylome, e.g. H. pylori J99 ONT native/WGA data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-motif-benchmarking-against-pacbio-reference))
- **methylation-motif-classification**: Use when Nanopore reads from bacteria yield candidate methylation motifs whose types (4mC/5mC/6mA) and methylated-base positions must be classified with a trained multi-column CNN, especially when nanodisco's mean-feature-only typing is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-motif-classification))
- **methylation-motif-concordance-comparison**: Use when a de novo methylation motif set from ONT sequencing data must be validated against a published reference methylome motif set (e.g. PacBio-derived H. pylori J99 motifs from Krebes et al. 2014) to quantify concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-motif-concordance-comparison))
- **methylation-motif-de-novo-detection**: Use when you have paired native and WGA-control ONT FAST5 read sets plus a reference genome and need to discover methylated sequence motifs de novo, without prior knowledge of methyltransferase specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-motif-de-novo-detection))
- **methylation-motif-sequence-analysis**: Use when you have resquiggled multi-fast5 ONT reads for a native sample and a matched WGA control plus a reference genome FASTA, and you need to discover methylated sequence motifs de novo rather than test known motifs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/methylation-motif-sequence-analysis))
- **methylation-object-merging-and-union**: Use when you have loaded individual methylation call files as methylRawList objects from bisulfite sequencing experiments (via methRead()) and need to perform base-level comparative analysis across two or more samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/methylation-object-merging-and-union))

- **methylation-pipeline-runtime-comparison**: Use when estimating or reproducing the computational cost of methylR's ChAMP or minfi methylation array pipelines on the paper's test dataset under stated hardware constraints, e.g. to plan capacity or validate the tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/methylation-pipeline-runtime-comparison))
- **methylation-probe-count-validation**: Use when immediately after loading raw methylation array data using champ.load() or champ.import() to verify data integrity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/methylation-probe-count-validation))
- **methylation-probe-variability-filtering**: Use when a buccal DNA methylation matrix (Infinium MethylationEPIC betas) must be reduced to its most variable autosomal probes as a preprocessing view for unsupervised multi-omics integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/methylation-probe-variability-filtering))
- **methylation-profile-significance-plotting**: Use when individually analyzed bisulfite sequencing PCR (.ab1 trace) samples must be gathered into groups (e.g. high- vs low-methylated DNA) and compared with t-tests plus per-CpG significance-annotated profile plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-profile-significance-plotting))
- **methylation-quantification-from-enrichment-data**: Use when you have cfMeDIP-seq (or MeDIP-seq) aligned enrichment data from raw FASTQs and need per-sample methylation quantification plus aggregated, filtered bin-by-sample methylation matrices across a cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/methylation-quantification-from-enrichment-data))
- **methylation-region-genomic-context-assignment**: Use when after identifying differentially methylated bases or regions (via calculateDiffMeth() and getMethylDiff()), when you need to characterize WHERE these methylation changes occur relative to gene structure and CpG density landscapes.
